       let onlyOnce;

       let source = urlParams.get('source');
       if(source === 'app'){
         document.getElementById("block-headerbsp").style.display = "none";
         document.getElementById("block-footerbsp").style.display = "none";
         document.getElementsByClassName("coh-container coh-ce-922fd972")[0].className += " source-app-container";
       }
    })(jQuery, Drupal);

<script type="text/javascript" nonce="6045d3769b8c0d401137c75a7c95ca09">
document.addEventListener('readystatechange', event => {
    (function ($) {//COD_APP
         $.noConflict();
        function adjustMarginBottom() {
            if (window.innerWidth < 768) {
                $(".template-container").addClass("app");
            } else {
                $(".template-container").removeClass("app");
            }
        }
        if ((event.target.readyState === 'interactive') && (window.location.search.includes("source=app"))) {
            if (window.innerWidth < 1280) {
                $("main").css("padding-top", "0");
                $("#hs-eu-cookie-confirmation").css("display", "none");
            } else {
                $("main").css("padding-top", "");
                $("#hs-eu-cookie-confirmation").css("display", "none");
            }
        }
        if (event.target.readyState === 'interactive') {
            document.addEventListener('click', e => {
                const origin = e.target.closest('a');
                if (window.location.search.includes("source=app")) {
                  if (origin.href && !origin.href.includes("source=app")) {
                    event.preventDefault();
                    event.stopPropagation();
                    origin.href += origin.href.includes("?") ? "&source=app" : "?source=app";
                    location.href = origin.href;
                    return false;
                  }
                }
            });
        }

       if (event.target.readyState === 'complete') {
           if (window.location.search.includes("source=app")) {
              adjustMarginBottom();
           }
            $(document).ajaxComplete(function(ev, jqXHR, settings) {
               if($(".ajax-progress.ajax-progress-fullscreen")) { $(".ajax-progress.ajax-progress-fullscreen").remove();}
               $(".overlay").css("display", "none");
               $("body").removeClass("modal-open coh-modal-overlay-open");
            });
          $('[id="edit-sort-bef-combine"],[id^="edit-sort-bef-combine--"]').click(function(){
                $(".search-form-container, .filters-container").css("display", "none");
                $(".searcher-button, .filters-button").removeClass("active");
                $(".sort-button").addClass("active");
                $(".overlay").css("display", "none");
          });
          $(".searcher-button").click(function(){
                $(".filters-container").css("display", "none");
                $(".filters-button, .sort-button").removeClass("active");
          });
          $(".filters-button").click(function(){
                $(".search-form-container").css("display", "none");
                $(".searcher-button, .sort-button").removeClass("active");
                $(".overlay").css("display", "none");
                $("body").removeClass("modal-open coh-modal-overlay-open");
          });
          $('.filters-container #edit-reset, .filters-container [id^="edit-reset--"]').attr("value","Limpiar filtros");
       }    
     })(jQuery, Drupal);
});

</script>

                       <script class="coh-inline-element ssa-component coh-component ssa-component-instance-b12f5152-ea39-4311-a549-a60c1177c129 coh-component-instance-b12f5152-ea39-4311-a549-a60c1177c129 app-script" nonce="6045d3769b8c0d401137c75a7c95ca09">document.addEventListener('readystatechange', event => {
    (function ($) {
      if (event.target.readyState === 'interactive') {
       // const cpt_banner_instance = document.querySelector('div.coh-component-instance-{{componentUuid}}');
const cpt_banner_instance = document.querySelector('.cpt-banner-app');
        cpt_banner_instance.querySelector('.coh-container-boxed').style.display = 'none';
        cpt_banner_instance.style.display = 'none';
        // Función para determinar que tipo de dispositivo es.
        const isMobile = {
          Android: () => navigator.userAgent.match(/Android/i),
          iOS: () => navigator.userAgent.match(/iPhone|iPad|iPod/i),
          any: () => (isMobile.Android() || isMobile.iOS()),
          isSafari: () => navigator.userAgent.match(/Version\/[\d\.]+.*Safari/),
        };
        // Objeto bannerApp y su funciones para mostrar, ocultar, remover y setear url.
        const bannerApp = {
          setUrl: function() {
            const enlace = cpt_banner_instance.querySelector('a');
            let url = enlace.getAttribute("data-android");
            if(isMobile.iOS()) {
              url = enlace.getAttribute("data-ios");
            }
            enlace.setAttribute("href", url);
          },
          show: function() {
            document.getElementsByTagName('main')[0] ? document.getElementsByTagName('main')[0].style.paddingTop = '140px' : null;
            document.querySelectorAll('.cpt-main-menu.mobile')[0] ? document.querySelectorAll('.cpt-main-menu.mobile')[0].classList.add('banner-app-reposition') : null;
            cpt_banner_instance.querySelector('.coh-container-boxed').style.display = 'flex';
            cpt_banner_instance.style.display = 'flex';
          },
          hide: function() {
            document.getElementsByTagName('main')[0] ? document.getElementsByTagName('main')[0].style.paddingTop = '60px' : null;
          },
          remove: function(banner) {
            cpt_banner_instance.remove();
          }
        }
        // Si no es Mobile o es Safari en iOS remover Banner.
        if (!isMobile.any()) {
          bannerApp.remove();
          return;
        }
        if (isMobile.any()) { 
          bannerApp.setUrl();
          bannerApp.show();
          document.querySelectorAll('.banner-close-btn')[0].addEventListener('click', function(e) {
            bannerApp.hide();
            bannerApp.remove();
          });
        }
      };

                                             <script class="coh-inline-element ssa-component coh-component ssa-component-instance-71f01bf5-4f5c-4b81-b986-0e0c6b4c32d1 coh-component-instance-71f01bf5-4f5c-4b81-b986-0e0c6b4c32d1" nonce="6045d3769b8c0d401137c75a7c95ca09">document.addEventListener('readystatechange', event => {
    (function ($, Drupal) {
      let onlyOnce;
      Drupal.behaviors.menu_beneficios = {
        attach: function attach(context, settings) {
          if (!onlyOnce) {
            onlyOnce = true;
            if (event.target.readyState === 'interactive') {
              let path = $(location).attr('pathname');
              if (path.split("/").length == 2) {
                path = "/" + path.split("/")[1];
                $('.container-icon-text[href="'+path+'"]').parent().addClass('active');
              } else if (path.split("/").length == 3) {
                $('.container-icon-text[href="'+path+'"]').parent().addClass('active');
              } else if (path.split("/").length == 4) {
                path = "/" + path.split("/")[1] + "/" + path.split("/")[2];
                $('.container-icon-text[href="'+path+'"]').parent().addClass('active');
              }
            }
            $("a.container-icon-text").on("click", function(e) {
                if($(this).parent().hasClass("active")) {
                    e.preventDefault();
                } else {
                    $("ul.container-list-beneficios li.active").removeClass("active");
                }
            });
          }
        },
      };
    })(jQuery, Drupal);
  });</script>  <div class="coh-container ssa-component coh-component ssa-component-instance-71f01bf5-4f5c-4b81-b986-0e0c6b4c32d1 coh-component-instance-71f01bf5-4f5c-4b81-b986-0e0c6b4c32d1     (function ($) {
        function modifyHrefForApp() {
            const links = document.getElementsByTagName("a");
            for (let i = 0; i < links.length; i++) {
              let href = links[i].getAttribute("href");
              if (href && href.includes("source=app")) {
                continue;
              }
              if (href && href.includes("?")) {
                 href += "&source=app";
              } else {
                 href += "?source=app";
              }
              links[i].setAttribute("href", href);
            }
            if ($('[data-drupal-selector="edit-reset"]')) {
                $('[data-drupal-selector="edit-reset"]').on("click", function(e) {
                    e.preventDefault(); 
                    location.reload();
                });
            }
          }

          function adjustMarginBottom() {
            if (window.innerWidth < 768) {
                $(".template-container").addClass("app");
            } else {
                $(".template-container").removeClass("app");
            }
          }

       if (event.target.readyState === 'complete') {
           if (window.location.search.includes("source=app")) {
              modifyHrefForApp();
              adjustMarginBottom();
           }
          $(document).ajaxComplete(function(ev, jqXHR, settings) {
            if ((settings.url == "/views/ajax?_wrapper_format=drupal_ajax") || (settings.url.includes("source=app"))){
                if (settings.url.includes("source=app")) {
                    modifyHrefForApp();
                }
               if($(".ajax-progress.ajax-progress-fullscreen")) {
                  $(".ajax-progress.ajax-progress-fullscreen").remove();
               }
            }
            $(".overlay").css("display", "none");
            $("body").removeClass("modal-open coh-modal-overlay-open");
          });
          $('[id="edit-sort-bef-combine"],[id^="edit-sort-bef-combine--"]').click(function(){
          $(".search-form-container, .filters-container").css("display", "none");
          $(".searcher-button, .filters-button").removeClass("active");
          $(".sort-button").addClass("active");
          $(".overlay").css("display", "none");
          });
          $(".searcher-button").click(function(){
          $(".filters-container").css("display", "none");
          $(".filters-button, .sort-button").removeClass("active");
          });
          $(".filters-button").click(function(){
          $(".search-form-container").css("display", "none");
          $(".searcher-button, .sort-button").removeClass("active");
          $(".overlay").css("display", "none");
          $("body").removeClass("modal-open coh-modal-overlay-open");
          });
          $('.filters-container #edit-reset, .filters-container [id^="edit-reset--"]').attr("value","Limpiar filtros");
       }    
     })(jQuery, Drupal);
});</script>
